# Sound audit

Every sound event of ours, judged against the best we have (SPEC §66). Written by `python3 tools/art/sound_audit.py audit`
from the tables in `tools/art/specs/audit_*.py`, so it is always the game as built. Do not edit it by hand.

Asked for by Euan on 11 Nov 2026: "double check basically all the custom sounds we have and do a full audit and make
sure they're all on par with the best sounds we have". The same day, on mining: "I hate the ding ding ding when mining
ore, even vanilla sounds better".

**The bar.** The armour sounds (SPEC §44.12) and the Forged Pick (SPEC §49): Minecraft's own recordings, named in
`sounds.json`, layered in code. A sound is at the bar when it is a recording of the real thing, belongs in Minecraft's
world, and has no narrow steady high tone at its peak (`docs/powers-aaa/taster/ding_check.py`). A sound built by
synthesis (sine partials, filtered noise, voiced formants) is below it, however well its loudness and brightness match.

**The count.** 1890 events: 1003 at the bar, 122 remade in this audit, 765 left as they are (each row says why).

**How to read a row.** *Source before* is where the sound came from before the audit. *Fix, as built* is the recipe the
game plays now: each recording by its name in the game, `×` its volume and `@` its pitch, `+ms` when it starts late.
A recipe's first recording is the event in `sounds.json`; the rest are layered by `core.audio.client.SoundLayers`.
Every remade sound has up to three picks on the listening page (`python3 tools/art/sound_audit.py listen`); the row
shows the one in the game.


## Birds and wings

What you hear on a beach, in a wood and in the city's squares when a bird takes off, calls or sings.

Synthetic, and the loudest of the bird sounds (peaks near full scale) | A flock: six or seven parrot wingbeats tumbling over each other, over a bat's take-off. `mob/parrot/fly1 ..` ×1 @0.95; `entity.bat.takeoff` ×0.26 @0.9; `entity.parrot.fly` ×1 @1.1 +50ms; `entity.parrot.fly` ×1 @0.9 +150ms; `entity.parrot.fly` ×0.9 @1.05 +250ms; `entity.bat.takeoff` ×0.16 @1.05 +200ms; `entity.parrot.fly` ×0.8 @0.85 +400ms; `entity.parrot.fly` ×0.7 @1 +600ms; `entity.parrot.fly` ×0.5 @0.9 +850ms |

## Mining

What you hear with a pick in ore. Nothing here is a note any more: stone, grit and metal, the game's own.

| Event | What it is | Where and when it plays | Source before | Verdict | Fix, as built |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `delve.strike.coal` | The pick bites coal | delve.client.StrikeFeel.bite (client only): every fourth tick while you dig this ore, under the game's own digging sound, and louder on the blow that breaks it | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): struck sine modes and noise | Below the bar. Synthetic thock | Tuff knocked, lowered: soft and crumbly. `block.tuff.hit` ×0.6 @0.85 |
| `delve.strike.copper` | The pick bites copper | delve.client.StrikeFeel.bite (client only): every fourth tick while you dig this ore, under the game's own digging sound, and louder on the blow that breaks it | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): struck sine modes and noise | Below the bar. Synthetic, with a held tone at 950 Hz | A dense metal knock (the heavy core's), a little raised: dull, no ring. `block.heavy_core.hit` ×0.6 @1.15 |
| `delve.strike.iron` | The pick bites iron | delve.client.StrikeFeel.bite (client only): every fourth tick while you dig this ore, under the game's own digging sound, and louder on the blow that breaks it | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): struck sine modes and noise | Below the bar. A ding: 92% of the sound one tone at 1,174 Hz | The same dense metal knock, lower and heavier. `block.heavy_core.hit` ×0.7 @0.9 |

| `delve.true_strike` | A true strike: the pick lands on the seam | delve.client.StrikeFeel.trueStrike: when a bite lands on the ore's seam; a little brighter with each one in a row (the note it used to climb is gone: StrikeFeel now asks for a small rise only) | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): struck sine modes at the ore's note | Below the bar. The ding itself: 89% one tone, climbing two semitones with every hit in a row | Minecraft's critical-hit crack, quiet, with a split of stone: a clean hit, no note. `entity.player.attack.crit` ×0.45 @1; `block.deepslate.break` ×0.3 @1.25 |
| `delve.fork_ring` | The Tuning Fork rings toward a lode | delve.client.ForkSong: a ring that lasts a few seconds after the strike and climbs in pitch as you face the lode (the game bends it) | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): a pure sine loop | Below the bar. A pure synthetic tone, looped | Amethyst resonating: the game's own long ring, struck again as it fades. `block.amethyst_block.resonate` ×1 @1 |
| `delve.piton_set` | A piton hammered into rock | delve.PitonBlock: placing a piton | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): a struck sine tink | Below the bar. Synthetic tink | A chain set down on stone, with the stone knocked. `block.chain.place` ×0.7 @0.9; `block.stone.hit` ×0.8 @0.8 |
| `delve.rope_pay` | Rope pays out from a piton | delve.PitonBlock: using a piton to pay out its rope | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): band noise whir | Below the bar. Synthetic whir | A fishing line reeled, lowered, with a lead let go. `entity.fishing_bobber.retrieve` ×0.8 @0.7; `item.lead.untied` ×0.6 @0.9 |
| `delve.deepglass_hum` | Deepglass hums | delve.DeepglassBlock (client, a random tick of the block near you) | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): sine partials | Below the bar. Synthetic hum | A beacon's hum, raised and quiet. `block.beacon.ambient` ×0.35 @1.4 |
| `delve.deep_ear_cave` | Deep Ear: a cave breathes behind the rock | delve.client (the Deep Ear's tell when a cave lies behind the wall you face) | ours, synthesised (tools/art/specs/audio_delve.py): filtered noise | Below the bar. Synthetic breath of noise | A breeze drawing breath, lowered. `entity.breeze.inhale` ×0.5 @0.6 |

Synthetic hum | Amethyst resonating, well lowered: a hum through stone. `block.amethyst_block.resonate` ×0.5 @0.55 |

## The world's places and ambience

Caves, wind, water, far happenings, rifts and fallen stars, the special places, the city at work, the Hearth.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `anomaly.rift_open` | A rift tears open | world.anomaly: a rift appearing | ours, synthesised (audio_discovery.py: noise tear) | Below the bar. Synthetic tear (noise) | Glass breaking, slowed, with a portal's trigger. `block.glass.break` ×0.6 @0.6; `block.portal.trigger` ×0.35 @1.3 |

Synthetic shimmer | A beacon's hum, raised and quiet. `block.beacon.ambient` ×0.3 @1.6 |
